pattern-fix (record per IMPROVE THE MACHINE): this run's dependency audit table header read `| package | upstream deps |` while `gate_passes()` in `.github/workflows/opencode-schedule.yml` demands the exact string `| package | upstream deps found |`, and `tools/verify-fedora.sh` checked neither that header nor the `status: complete` line. So the verifier could report PASS while the workflow gate rejected the same block, which is what has been failing recent runs as "autonomous run failed" with a complete-looking relay. Fixed both sides this session: header corrected in this run's block (commit ed062f3), verifier now carries the gate's two missing checks (commit 993d53a). Harness lesson relearned: container-teardown.sh takes the bare spec filename (it already cd's into the copied package dir). Dir-prefixed spec paths fail builddep with file-not-found. Early parallel failures this pass were that caller error, not spec bugs. Portal --help exits 1 with no session bus, so its smoke is an ldd link check. Protonplus takes a `version` subcommand, not --version. Heroic needs seccomp=unconfined plus non-root xvfb launch (rc=124 alive, zero setuid lines); plain docker gives a namespace artifact crash, which is not a packaging bug.
